# Arsip Penuh: Tabel-Tabel Komposisi Bahan Makanan Ternak untuk Indonesia (Hartadi et al., 1980)

- **Sumber Asli:** `Hartadi, H., S. Reksohadiprodjo, S. Lebdosukojo, A. D. Tillman, L. C. Kearl, & L. E. Harris (November 1980).` _Tabel-Tabel dari Komposisi Bahan Makanan Ternak untuk Indonesia / Tables of Feed Composition for Indonesia._ Fakultas Peternakan UGM Yogyakarta & International Feedstuffs Institute, Utah State University.
- **PDF Lokal:** `docs/references/Hartadi-1980-Tabel-Komposisi-Pakan-Indonesia.pdf` (153 halaman)
- **JSON Tervalidasi:** `docs/references/hartadi-1980-tables.json`
- **Total Entri Terekstrak (100% Tanpa Pemangkasan):** Tabel 12 = 246 bahan · Tabel 13 = 176 bahan · Tabel 14 = 64 bahan · Tabel 15 = 44 suplemen mineral.

## 1. Simbol Catatan Kaki & Aturan Summarization Data (hlm. 20–27)

- `*` : Nilai estimasi hasil persamaan regresi (Tabel 9, Tabel 10, atau konversi TDN/DE/ME/NEl).
- `+` : Data Amerika Serikat/Kanada disisipkan karena data analisis Indonesia tidak tersedia.
- `&` : Nilai terhitung (_calculated values_) berbasis data Amerika Serikat/Kanada.
- `NFE (%)` = `100 - Ash (%) - Crude Fiber (%) - Ether Extract (%) - Crude Protein (%)` (dihitung pada basis 100% BK sebelum dikonversi ke As-Fed).

## 2. Kelas Bahan Makanan Internasional — Tabel 7

| Kode | Nama Indonesia                                     | Nama Inggris                               | Definisi Basis BK                                                                                 |
| ---- | -------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1    | Hijauan kering dan jerami                          | Dry forages and roughages                  | >18% CF or >35% cell wall (hay, straw, fodder, stover, hulls)                                     |
| 2    | Pasture, tanaman padangan, hijauan diberikan segar | Pasture, range plants, and feeds fed green | All green forages cut or grazed and fed fresh                                                     |
| 3    | Silase                                             | Silages                                    | Ensiled forages (corn, alfalfa, grass, etc.; excludes fish, grain, root, tuber silages)           |
| 4    | Sumber energi                                      | Energy feeds                               | <20% CP and <18% CF (or <35% cell wall); grains, milling by-products, fruits, nuts, roots, tubers |
| 5    | Sumber protein                                     | Protein supplements                        | >=20% CP and animal-origin feeds (including ensiled animal feeds), oilseed cakes/meals            |
| 6    | Sumber mineral                                     | Mineral supplements                        | Inorganic and bone mineral sources                                                                |
| 7    | Sumber vitamin                                     | Vitamin supplements                        | Vitamin concentrates and irradiated/yeast vitamin feeds                                           |
| 8    | Additives                                          | Additives                                  | Antibiotics, coloring/flavoring agents, hormones, medicants, and water                            |

## 3. Persamaan Energi, TDN, Protein Tercerna & Konversi Vitamin A

- **DE Sapi & Domba:** `*DE (Mcal/kg) = TDN% * 0.04409` · `*TDN% = DE / 0.04409` (Crampton et al., 1957; Swift, 1957)
- **DE Kuda (Kelas 1):** `*DE (Mcal/kg) = 0.0365 * TDN% + 0.172` · `*TDN% = 20.35 * DE + 8.90` (Fonnesbeck et al., 1967; Fonnesbeck, 1968)
- **DE Babi:** `*DE (kcal/kg) = TDN% * 44.09`
- **ME Sapi & Domba:** `*ME (Mcal/kg) = -0.45 + 1.01 * DE (Mcal/kg)` · `*TDN% = 27.65 * ME` (Moe & Tyrrell, 1976; Crampton/Swift)
- **ME Kuda:** `*ME (Mcal/kg) = 0.82 * DE (Mcal/kg)`
- **ME Babi:** `*ME (kcal/kg) = (0.96 - 0.00202 * CP%) * DE (kcal/kg)` (Asplund & Harris, 1969 — terverifikasi dari hlm. 34 Inggris; teks Indonesia hlm. 20 mengandung salah cetak 0.92 & 5)
- **NE Sapi Potong (Garrett, 1977; dengan `ME = 0.82 * DE`):**
  - `NEm (Mcal/kg) = 1.115 - 0.8971*ME + 0.6507*ME^2 - 0.1028*ME^3 + 0.005725*ME^4`
  - `NEg (Mcal/kg) = 3.178*ME - 0.8646*ME^2 + 0.1275*ME^3 - 0.006787*ME^4 - 3.325`
- **NE Laktasi (Moe & Tyrrell, 1976):** `*NEl (Mcal/kg) = -0.12 + 0.0245 * TDN%`
- **Standar Vitamin A:** `1 IU = 0.344 ug all-trans vit A asetat = 0.300 ug retinol = 0.550 ug palmitat = 0.6 ug beta-karotena (standar tikus; 1 mg = 1667 IU)`.

## 4. Catatan Salah Cetak Buku Asli (Preserved Verbatim di Arsip)

- **T12 (0433/0434):** Printed DM% for As-Fed is 1.% (likely printer drop from ~86.%); computerized DM-basis columns on row 0434 are ~100x inflated (Ash 1127.9%, CP 534.9%). Preserved verbatim in archive; normalized safely in production TS library.
- **T12 (0457/0458):** Printed CF% shows 99.5% AF / 115.7% DM (printer decimal shift from ~9.95% / 11.57%). Preserved verbatim in archive; corrected in production TS library using AFprox check.
- **T13 (0037/0038):** Printed Cu (mg/kg) shows 54.33 AF / 73.02 DM despite 20% DM. Preserved verbatim.
- **T13 (0047/0048):** Printed Zn (mg/kg) shows 20.70 AF / 31.63 DM at 92% DM. Preserved verbatim.
- **T13 (0175/0176):** Printed p.125 placed 98.00 / 00.00 in Biotin column (printer format-wrap from Choline 3698. / 4300.). Preserved in archive with note.
- **T13 (0325/0326):** Printed Zn (mg/kg) shows 10.11+ AF / 28.04+ DM at 86% DM. Preserved verbatim.
- **T15 (065/066):** Printed NaF Sodium % shows smudge E4.75 (54.75% Na per NaF stoichiometry).
